# THE MACHINE

### Book 1 of two

---

## Yeh kitaab aapko batati nahi hai

Zyada kitaabein aapko ek definition deti hain aur ummeed karti hain ki aap maan lo. Aap padhte ho, sar hilate ho, aur ek hafte baad kuch bacha nahi hota.

Aap yeh already jaante ho ki yeh tareeka aap pe kaam nahi karta.

Toh yeh kitaab ulta chalti hai.

Har chapter aapke saamne ek problem rakhta hai. Asli problem, jismein log hote hain. Phir poochta hai ki aap kya karte. Aap sochte ho. Aap kuch nikaalte ho. Aur phir kitaab batati hai ki jo cheez aapne abhi banayi, woh already exist karti hai, aur duniya use is naam se bulati hai.

Aap naam yaad nahi kar rahe. Aap cheezein bana rahe ho, aur naam baad mein utha rahe ho.

Knowledge sirf isi tarah se tikta hai.

---

## Do log

Yeh kitaab do logon ki baat-cheet se chalti hai.

**Madhav.** Umar saath ke aas-paas. Poori zindagi cheezein banayi hain, pehle machine, phir software. Ab padhata hai. Jawab seedha nahi deta, kyunki uska maanna hai ki jo jawab diya jaaye woh tikta nahi.

**Kabir.** Umar pachchees. Padhai poori ho chuki hai. Kai cheezein shuru ki hain, koi khatam nahi hui. Roz AI istemaal karta hai aur use bilkul samajh nahi aata ki andar kya hai. Har hafte kisi nayi cheez ka naam sunta hai jo baaki sabko pehle se pata tha. Woh sust nahi hai, aur woh bewakoof toh bilkul nahi hai. Uske paas bahut saare tukde hain jo aapas mein judte nahi.

Kabir aksar galat hota hai. Uske galat jawab iss kitaab ka sabse kaam ka hissa hain, kyunki woh wahi galtiyan hain jo lagbhag har koi karta hai. Jab woh galat ho, toh ruk kar socho ki aap bhi wahi sochte the ya nahi.

---

## Har chapter do hisson mein hai

```
SAMVAAD       Madhav aur Kabir baat karte hain. Yahan idea
              banta hai. Isse padho jaise aap kamre mein baithe ho.

USKE BAAD     Naam, asli duniya ka example, log kya galat samajhte
              hain, map, aur sawal-jawab. Yeh hissa seedha likha hai
              taaki baad mein dhoondhne mein aasan ho.
```

Samvaad mein aap seekhte ho. Uske baad wala hissa isliye hai ki chhe mahine baad jab aapko kuch yaad karna ho, toh poora baat-cheet dobara na padhna pade.

---

## Chaar vaade

**Koi shabd aapki zaroorat se pehle nahi aayega.** Aap kabhi aisa word nahi milenge jiski kami aapne pehle mehsoos na ki ho. Agar koi word bold mein dikhe, toh matlab do page pehle aapne woh idea bana liya tha, aur ab bas uska label mil raha hai.

**Bina wajah kuch exist nahi karta.** Technology ki har cheez kisi insaan ne banayi thi, jiske paas ek problem thi. Yeh kitaab hamesha pehle problem deti hai. Agar kabhi kuch bewajah lage, toh woh likhne ki galti hai, aapki nahi.

**Ek chapter, ek idea.** Chapters jaan-boojh ke chote hain. Do ya teen page. Ek nayi cheez. Agar chapter khatam karke aap us ek idea ko bol sako, toh woh chapter kaam kar gaya.

**Baat hamesha aap tak wapas aati hai.** Har chapter aise khatam hota hai jo aapne khud dekha hai, aksar isi hafte.

---

## Do kitaabein, aur yeh pehli kyun hai

```
BOOK 1   THE MACHINE
         Switches, languages, networks, data, serving.
         Jo 2005 mein sach tha aur 2045 mein bhi sach rahega.
         Yeh kitaab kabhi purani nahi hogi.

BOOK 2   THE THINKING MACHINE
         Probability, learning, meaning, transformers, agents.
         Yeh har saal thodi badalti rahegi.
```

Book 2 zyada exciting lagti hai, aur log wahin se shuru karna chahte hain. Problem yeh hai ki Book 2 ka har chapter Book 1 ki cheezon pe khada hai. AI ek file hai numbers ki, jo ek machine pe chalti hai, ek network ke through aati hai, aur ek database se judti hai.

Pehle machine, phir thinking machine. Us order mein kuch bhi udhaar nahi lena padta.

---

## Kitni tezi se padhein

58 chapters. Do chapter roz padho toh chaar hafte. Ek roz padho toh do mahine.

Ek baithak mein poora khatam mat karo. Yeh kitaab is tarah likhi gayi hai ki har chapter ke baad rukna pade. Jo minute aap ruk ke sochte ho, asli kitaab wahin hoti hai.

---

## Do tarah ke chapters

```
[SPINE]   Poora map isse banta hai. Yeh sab padho.
[DEPTH]   Mechanism andar se. Pehli baar mein chhod sakte ho.
```

Sirf SPINE padhoge toh 37 chapters hain, aur poora naksha phir bhi mil jaayega. DEPTH wale tab padho jab jaanna ho ki andar se kaam kaise karta hai.

---

## Sawal jo chapter ke aakhir mein aate hain

Har chapter do ya teen sawal ke saath khatam hota hai. Teen tarah ke sawal hain:

```
SAMAJH CHECK    is chapter ka idea pakda ya nahi
JODNE WALA      yeh chapter pehle wale kis cheez se judta hai
DESIGN SAWAL    ek part mein ek baar. Kuch banane ka sawal.
```

Yeh test nahi hain. Computer ki zaroorat nahi. Likhne ki bhi nahi, sirf design wale sawal ke liye.

Ek minute baitho har sawal pe, aage badhne se pehle.

Har sawal ka jawab usi ke neeche likha hai. Pehle apna jawab socho, sahi ho ya galat, phir neeche padho. Galat jawab jo aapne socha, woh sahi jawab se behtar hai jo aapko diya gaya.

Design sawalon ke jawab `WORKBOOK.md` mein likhna. Woh iss kitaab ka asli output hai.

---

## Ek cheez jo poori kitaab mein chalti rahegi

Chapter 1.1 mein hum ek chhota product uthayenge: **ek chat app jo aapke saare messages store karta hai aur unmein se dhoondh sakta hai.**

Simple lagta hai. Har part mein hum uspe wapas aayenge, aur har baar pata chalega ki uske andar ek aur layer chhupi thi.

Kitaab ke aakhir mein aap us app ko poora khol chuke honge, upar se neeche tak.

---

## Ek hi instruction

Aage mat kudo.

Har chapter pichhle wale pe khada hai. Iss kitaab mein jargon nahi hai, jiska matlab yeh bhi hai ki beech mein se ghus ke catch up karne ka koi rasta nahi hai. Chain hi poora design hai.

Page palto.

---

# Chapter 0.1  [SPINE]
## The village: paisa exist hi kyun karta hai

---

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ek gaon socho. Sau log, ek valley mein. Paani hai, zameen hai, patthar hai, lakdi hai. Paisa nahi hai, kyunki abhi tak kisi ne invent hi nahi kiya. Sabko khaana chahiye, paani chahiye, chhat chahiye, bimari mein ilaaj chahiye. Ab batao, woh jiyenge kaise?

**Kabir:** Har banda apna kaam kar lega. Apna khaana ugayega, apna ghar banayega.

**Madhav:** Achha. Toh jis din uski haddi tootegi, us din?

**Kabir:** Woh... khud jod lega?

**Madhav:** Kisne sikhaya use haddi jodna?

**Kabir:** Theek hai. Woh kaam nahi karega.

**Madhav:** Aur bhi kuch nahi chalega. Ek aadmi saat kaam kare toh saaton kharaab karega. Din mein itni roshni hi nahi hai. Gaon zinda toh rahega, lekin jaanwaron ke level pe. Toh doosra tareeka batao.

**Kabir:** Kaam baant lein. Bees kheti karein, das paani laayein, bees ghar banayein, das ilaaj seekhein.

**Madhav:** Haan. Ab dhyaan do ki kya hua. Gaon ko nayi zameen mili?

**Kabir:** Nahi.

**Madhav:** Naye auzaar? Naye log?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Kuch bhi naya nahi mila.

**Madhav:** Phir bhi ab woh pehle se zyada banate hain. Sirf ek arrangement badla, aur output badh gaya. Yeh yaad rakhna, aage kaam aayega. Ab agla sawal. Kisan ke paas anaaj hai, chhat nahi. Mistri ke paas hunar hai, khaane ko kuch nahi. Kya karenge?

**Kabir:** Aasan hai. Kisan anaaj dega, mistri chhat banayega.

**Madhav:** Bilkul. Ab vaidya. Usne mochi ka haath theek kiya. Vaidya ko joote chahiye.

**Kabir:** Toh mochi joote de dega. Hisaab barabar.

**Madhav:** Aur teen mahine baad vaidya ko phir joote chahiye. Iss baar mochi bilkul theek-thaak hai. Usko vaidya se kuch nahi chahiye.

**Kabir:** Toh vaidya intezaar karega ki mochi bimar pade.

**Madhav:** Toh vaidya ki roti iss baat pe tiki hai ki log bimar padein?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Yeh toh galat hai.

**Madhav:** Galat hai, aur yeh koi ajeeb case bhi nahi hai. Master ko anaaj chahiye lekin kisan ke bachche bade ho gaye. Pehredaar ko chhat chahiye lekin mistri ka ghar bana hua hai. Seedha exchange sirf tab chalta hai jab do log ek hi waqt pe ek doosre ka kaam chahte hon.

**Kabir:** Toh likh lo. Ek register banao. Vaidya ne mochi ka kaam kiya, likh do. Baad mein mochi se le lega.

**Madhav:** Achha jawab. Sach mein achha, aur duniya ne yeh kiya bhi hai. Lekin do sawal. Register rakhega kaun?

**Kabir:** Koi ek aadmi.

**Madhav:** Aur jis din usne apna naam thoda bada likh diya?

**Kabir:** Hmm.

**Madhav:** Doosra sawal. Sau log the toh register chal gaya. Ab das hazaar log hain, alag gaon hain, log aate jaate hain. Kitne register?

**Kabir:** Bahut zyada. Aur kisi ko kisi pe bharosa bhi nahi hoga.

**Madhav:** Toh register ka idea sahi hai, uski shakal galat hai. Ab socho. Aisi kaunsi cheez ho jo mochi le lega, chahe use ilaaj bilkul nahi chahiye?

**Kabir:** Aisi cheez jo uske kaam ki ho. Anaaj?

**Madhav:** Kabhi kabhi. Aur jis din uske paas anaaj bhara ho?

**Kabir:** Toh phir... aisi cheez jo woh kisi aur ko de sake.

**Madhav:** Ruko. Yeh important hai. Woh use kyun lega?

**Kabir:** Kyunki use pata hai ki baaki sab bhi lenge.

**Madhav:** Bas. Woh cheez uske kisi kaam ki nahi hai. Woh use isliye leta hai kyunki use bharosa hai ki aage badha dega. Ab batao, us cheez mein kya kya hona chahiye?

**Kabir:** Sadni nahi chahiye. Warna jab tak main use karun, khatam.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Uthane mein aasan ho. Bhaari patthar se kaam nahi chalega.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Nakli banana mushkil ho. Warna koi bhi bana lega aur bina kuch diye sab le lega.

**Madhav:** Aur? Ek chhoti cheez khareedni ho toh?

**Kabir:** Toh woh tukdon mein bat jaani chahiye.

**Madhav:** Aur aakhri, sabse zaroori?

**Kabir:** Sab log use maanein. Ek bhi na maane toh chain toot jaati hai.

**Madhav:** Tumne abhi paisa invent kar diya.

---

### Naam

Jo cheez Kabir ne nikaali, uska naam hai **paisa**.

Ab dhyaan do ki woh actually hai kya, kyunki yahin par lagbhag sab log galat samajhte hain.

Paisa daulat nahi hai. Paisa **ek record hai ki aapne kisi ko value di**, jise aap gaon mein kisi ke bhi saamne rakh sakte ho.

Anaaj daulat hai. Chhat daulat hai. Judi hui haddi daulat hai. Tokens doosre logon ke kaam pe ek claim hain.

Aur Kabir ka register wala jawab galat nahi tha. Paisa hi ek register hai, bas woh kisi ek aadmi ke paas nahi rakha. Woh sabke haathon mein tukdon mein baant diya gaya hai. Isiliye use kisi rakhwaale pe bharose ki zaroorat nahi padti.

Ek doosri cheez bhi paisa chupke se karta hai. Jo aadmi leta hai lekin deta kuch nahi, uske paas kuch jama nahi hota. Paise se pehle yeh dikhna mushkil tha.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

1945 mein ek British arthshastri, R. A. Radford, ne apne war-time prisoner camp ke baare mein likha. Har kaidi ko Red Cross se ek jaisa parcel milta tha: cigarettes, jam, butter, chocolate, biscuits.

Kisi ne paisa jaari nahi kiya. Koi sarkar nahi thi. Phir bhi kuch hi hafton mein cigarettes paisa ban gaye.

Aur exactly wahi hua jo samvaad mein nikla. Jo aadmi smoke nahi karta tha woh bhi cigarettes leta tha, kyunki use pata tha ki baaki sab lenge. Log cigarettes mein daam bolne lage: ek shirt itni cigarettes, ek chocolate utni. Jab parcel late aate the toh cigarettes kam ho jaati thin aur har cheez sasti ho jaati thi. Jab zyada aate the toh daam chadh jaate the.

Kisi ne yeh design nahi kiya. Kuch sau log, ek jagah band, aur exchange ki problem ne apne aap paisa bana diya. Aur usne wahi paanch shartein poori kin jo Kabir ne ginayi thin.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **paise ko hi daulat samajh lena.**

Isse ek khatarnak nateeja nikalta hai. Log sochte hain ki agar sabke paas zyada paisa aa jaaye toh sab ameer ho jaayenge. Sarkarein bhi kabhi kabhi yehi sochti hain.

Lekin paisa ek claim hai, cheez nahi. Zyada claim banane se cheezein nahi banti. Sirf har claim chhota ho jaata hai.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai kyunki ek aadmi ke level pe woh sach lagti hai. Agar sirf aapke paas zyada paisa aa jaaye, toh aap sach mein zyada cheezein khareed sakte ho. Dimaag ise sab pe laagu kar deta hai. Lekin ek aadmi ka zyada paisa doosron ke claims ka hissa cheen kar aata hai. Sabka zyada paisa kisi se kuch nahi cheenta, aur isliye kuch deta bhi nahi.

---

### Map pe

```
      ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
      │  aap woh dete ho jo logon ko chahiye     │
      └──────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                         ↓
      ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
      │  aapko tokens milte hain (uska record)   │
      └──────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                         ↓
      ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
      │  tokens doosron ke kaam pe claim hain    │
      └──────────────────────────────────────────┘

      Value nahi di, toh tokens nahi.
      Iss rule ka aaj tak koi exception nahi mila.
```

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Raat-o-raat gaon ke har aadmi ke paas das guna zyada tokens aa jaate hain. Anaaj utna hi hai, ghar utne hi hain, joote utne hi hain. Cheezon ka daam kya hoga? Aapka jawab tokens ke baare mein kya batata hai?

> **Jawab:** Daam lagbhag das guna ho jaayenge. Kisi ka kuch nahi badla.
>
> Wajah: tokens ka koi apna kaam nahi hai. Woh sirf iss baat ke record hain ki gaon ki cheezon pe aapka kitna hissa hai. Cheezein utni hi hain, toh hissa bhi utna hi hai. Sirf ginti ke number bade ho gaye.
>
> Isse ek aur baat nikalti hai: **paisa gaon ki daulat nahi hai. Woh us daulat ko baantne ka tareeka hai.** Camp wale example mein bilkul yehi dikha, jahan zyada cigarettes aate hi har cheez mehngi ho gayi.

**2. (samajh check)** Ek aadmi ko tokens ka bada dher viraasat mein milta hai aur woh zindagi bhar kisi ko koi value nahi deta. Woh khaata hai, ghar mein rehta hai, bimari mein ilaaj paata hai. Kya use gaon se asli value mil rahi hai? Agar haan, toh woh value banayi kisne, aur gaon ko badle mein kya mila?

> **Jawab:** Haan, use bilkul asli value mil rahi hai. Woh anaaj kisi kisan ne ugaya, woh ghar kisi mistri ne banaya.
>
> Value kisi aur ne banayi, aur gaon ko iss aadmi se badle mein kuch nahi mila.
>
> Lekin dhyaan do: value kisi waqt di zaroor gayi thi, sirf iss aadmi ne nahi di. Jisne di, usne woh record iss aadmi ko de diya. Tokens ki khaas baat yahi hai, ki woh haath badal sakte hain. Woh yeh nahi batate ki kaam kisne kiya, sirf yeh ki record ab kiske paas hai.
>
> Yeh paise ki kamzori hai, bug nahi. Agar tokens haath na badal sakein toh mochi wala deadlock wapas aa jaayega.

**3. (samajh check)** Paise ne mochi wala deadlock saaf kar diya. Ek aisi problem batao jo gaon ke paas paise se pehle nahi thi, aur ab hai.

> **Jawab:** Kai sahi jawab hain. Kuch mazboot wale:
>
> **Jama karna.** Anaaj sad jaata tha, isliye koi use jama nahi kar sakta tha. Tokens sadte nahi, isliye ab ek aadmi bahut saara hissa rok ke rakh sakta hai jabki baaki ke paas kam ho.
>
> **Kaam se kat jaana.** Pehle dikhta tha ki kisne kya diya. Ab sirf ek number reh gaya. Tokens yaad nahi rakhte ki woh kaise aaye. Chori aur imaandaari ke tokens ek jaise dikhte hain.
>
> **Nakli banana.** Ab shoonya se claim banane ka matlab hai gaon ki daulat ka hissa bina kuch diye le lena.
>
> **Paise ke liye kaam.** Pehle aap us cheez ke liye kaam karte the jo kisi ko chahiye thi. Ab aap tokens ke liye kaam kar sakte ho, chahe jo bana rahe ho woh kisi ke kaam ka ho ya na ho.

# Chapter 0.2  [SPINE]
## Same hours, different pay

---

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Usi gaon mein do log hain. Ravi paani dhota hai, sooraj se pehle nikalta hai, andhere mein lautta hai, bees saal se. Ek din chhutti nahi li. Uska ghar chhota hai. Meera haddi jodti hai. Kuch hafte chaar ghante kaam karti hai, kuch hafte bilkul nahi. Uska ghar gaon mein sabse bada hai.

**Kabir:** Toh gaon Ravi ke saath galat kar raha hai.

**Madhav:** Kaun kar raha hai galat? Naam batao.

**Kabir:** Panchayat.

**Madhav:** Koi panchayat nahi hai. Kisi ne rate tay nahi kiye. Har aadmi apni marzi se de raha hai.

**Kabir:** Toh phir aisa hua kaise?

**Madhav:** Yehi sawal hai. Teen tareeke se dekhte hain. Pehla: Ravi aaj paani na laaye toh tumhara kya jaayega?

**Kabir:** Mujhe khud nadi tak jaana padega. Ek ghanta.

**Madhav:** Aur Meera aaj tumhari tooti haddi na jode toh?

**Kabir:** Haddi tedhi jud jaayegi.

**Madhav:** Kitne din ke liye?

**Kabir:** Hamesha ke liye. Main zindagi bhar langdaunga.

**Madhav:** Toh ek ghanta, aur chaalis saal. Dono ne mehnat ki. Lekin dono ne jo cheez roki, woh barabar hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Bilkul nahi.

**Madhav:** Toh pehli baat: value mehnat se nahi aati. Woh us problem ke size se aati hai jo tum hatate ho. Ab doosra sawal. Paani gaon mein aur kitne log dho sakte hain?

**Kabir:** Sab. Matka uthana koi hunar nahi hai.

**Madhav:** Aur haddi kitne jod sakte hain?

**Kabir:** Sirf Meera.

**Madhav:** Ravi kal chhod de toh?

**Kabir:** Dopahar tak koi aur dho raha hoga.

**Madhav:** Meera kal chhod de toh?

**Kabir:** Das saal koi nahi hoga.

**Madhav:** Toh gaon ko Meera ko rokna padega. Ravi ko rokna zaroori nahi hai. Yeh zulm hai ya hisaab?

**Kabir:** Hisaab. Lekin bura lagta hai.

**Madhav:** Bura lagna aur galat hona alag cheezein hain. Ab teesra aadmi. Anil naam ka banda Ravi ko roz aate-jaate dekhta hai. Do saal lagakar woh nadi se gaon tak ek naali khodta hai. Do saal tak woh aisa kuch nahi banata jo koi pee sake. Phir naali poori ho jaati hai aur paani roz subah khud aata hai. Anil ghar chala jaata hai. Ab ginti karo. Ravi ne kitne saal kaam kiya?

**Kabir:** Bees.

**Madhav:** Uske bees saal ka kaam aaj kahan hai?

**Kabir:** Khatam. Jo paani dhoya woh pee liya gaya.

**Madhav:** Anil ne kitne saal kaam kiya?

**Kabir:** Do.

**Madhav:** Aur uske do saal ka kaam aaj kahan hai?

**Kabir:** Naali mein. Woh abhi bhi chal rahi hai.

**Madhav:** Anil ne Ravi se zyada mehnat ki?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Bahut kam ki.

**Madhav:** Toh farak kya tha?

**Kabir:** Anil ki mehnat ek cheez ke andar chali gayi, aur woh cheez uske bina bhi chalti rahi.

**Madhav:** Ab teeno ko saath rakho. Kisi ko kitna milega, yeh kya tay karta hai?

**Kabir:** Problem ka size. Kitne log woh kaam kar sakte hain. Aur kaam tikta hai ya khatam ho jaata hai.

**Madhav:** Teeno jodkar, ya guna karke?

**Kabir:** Guna. Kyunki agar ek bhi zero ho toh kuch nahi bachta. Anil ki naali se agar koi paani na peeta, toh woh kitni bhi durlabh hoti, uska kuch nahi milta.

**Madhav:** Sahi. Ab formula dobara bolo, aur mujhe batao ki usmein kya nahi hai.

**Kabir:** Size, guna kitne kam log, guna kitni baar wapas aata hai. Ghante usmein nahi hain.

**Madhav:** Ek baar bhi nahi. Aur yeh us gaon mein sach tha jahan na paisa tha, na malik, na koi economy. Yeh kisi ka banaya hua rule nahi hai. Yeh exchange se apne aap nikalta hai.

**Kabir:** Toh phir sab log yeh kyun maante hain ki ghanton ka paisa milta hai?

**Madhav:** Kyunki jab teeno dials neeche hon, tab ghante hi ek matra lever bachta hai. Aur jo ek matra lever bachta hai, woh mechanism lagne lagta hai.

---

### Naam

Teen cheezein, guna hokar:

```
        jo problem aap solve karte ho, uska size
    x   kitne kam log woh solve kar sakte hain
    x   aapka kaam kitni baar wapas aata rehta hai
    ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    =   aapko kitna milta hai
```

Doosri cheez ka naam hai **scarcity**. Teesri ka naam hai **leverage**.

Ghante formula mein nahi hain. Na guna, na jode, kahin nahi.

Lagbhag har koi maanta hai ki use ghanton ka paisa milta hai. Woh mechanism nahi hai. Woh woh hai jo bachta hai jab mechanism aapki pahunch se bahar ho.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

2014 mein Facebook ne WhatsApp ko lagbhag 19 billion dollar mein khareeda.

Us waqt WhatsApp mein **55 log** kaam karte the, jinmein sirf 32 engineers the. Aur unke app ko lagbhag 45 crore log har mahine istemaal karte the.

Ek engineer, lagbhag ek crore chalees lakh users.

Formula pe chalao. Size: logon ko ek doosre se baat karni hai, jo seedhi zaroorat hai. Scarcity: us waqt bahut kam log itne bade paimane pe chalne wala messaging system bana sakte the. Leverage: poora upar. Code ek baar likha gaya aur woh raat-din chalta raha, chahe likhne wala so raha ho.

Ab isi ke saath ek postman ki tulna karo, jo bhi sandesh pahunchane ka hi kaam karta hai. Wahi zaroorat. Postman roz chalta hai aur uska kaam har shaam khatam ho jaata hai.

Ravi aur Anil, doosri shakal mein. Field nahi badla. Position badli.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh maan lena ki sahi field chun lena hi asli faisla hai.**

Log saal-do-saal iss sawal pe atak jaate hain: doctor banun ya engineer, coding karun ya business. Jaise ek darwaza sahi ho aur baaki galat.

Lekin har field mein dono taraf ke log hain. Padhane mein bhi crore kamane wale hain aur mushkil se guzara karne wale bhi. Ilaaj mein bhi. Khaane mein bhi. Ek hi field ke andar ka farak, do alag fieldon ke farak se kahin bada hai.

```
PADHANA
  das students wala tutor        ←→  jiski kitaab ek crore log padhte hain

KHAANA
  ek kitchen mein cook            ←→  jiske do sau kitchen hain

ILAAJ
  roz tees mareez dekhne wala     ←→  jisne woh ilaaj banaya jo sab istemaal karte hain

SANGEET
  shaadi mein bajane wala          ←→  jiski recording sote waqt bajti rehti hai
```

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai kyunki field ka faisla **dikhta hai**. Uska ek naam hai, ek degree hai, log poochte hain. Position ka faisla dikhta nahi. Uska koi naam nahi hota aur woh ek din mein nahi hota, isliye lagta hai ki woh faisla hai hi nahi.

---

### Map pe

```
        KAM                                          ZYADA
        │                                              │
size    chhoti problem, bina bhi chal jaaye  ←→  badi problem, bina nahi chalti
scarce  bahut log kar sakte hain             ←→  bahut kam log kar sakte hain
leverage kaam karte hi khatam                ←→  kaam aapke bina bhi aata rehta hai
```

Teen dials. Jo milta hai woh teeno ka guna hai, isiliye ek dial ko neeche se upar karna sab kuch badal sakta hai, aur kisi dial ko chhue bina sirf zyada der kaam karne se lagbhag kuch nahi badalta.

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Ravi Anil ki naali dekhta hai aur sochta hai ki use aur mehnat karni chahiye. Woh bees ki jagah chalees matke roz dhone lagta hai aur uski kamai lagbhag dugni ho jaati hai. Meera aur Anil ki nahi badalti. Iss tareeke se uski kamai zyada se zyada kahan tak ja sakti hai, aur wahan ruk kyun jaati hai?

> **Jawab:** Ek din mein jitne matke ek insaan ka sharir dho sakta hai, bas wahan tak. Shayad saath, shayad assi. Uske baad kuch nahi.
>
> Ceiling ki wajah yeh hai ki Ravi apna **waqt** bech raha hai, aur waqt roz utna hi milta hai. Uska leverage dial zero pe atka hai, aur zero se guna karo toh mehnat kitni bhi ho, jawab chhota hi rehta hai.
>
> Yeh important isliye hai ki Ravi ka faisla galat nahi hai. Zyada mehnat se sach mein zyada milta hai. Bas woh raasta ek deewar pe khatam hota hai, aur woh deewar uske sharir mein hai. Anil ke raaste mein aisi koi deewar nahi thi.

**2. (samajh check)** Teen dials mein se kaunsa ek aadmi sabse jaldi badal sakta hai, aur kaunsa sabse dheere? Har ek ke liye actually kya chahiye, woh socho.

> **Jawab:** Sabse tez **problem ka size** hai. Aap kal se hi badi problem pe kaam karna shuru kar sakte ho. Kisi ki ijaazat nahi chahiye, sirf yeh chunna hai ki kis problem pe waqt lagana hai. Ravi agar kal se naali khodna shuru kare toh usne yeh dial usi din ghuma diya.
>
> Sabse dheema **scarcity** hai. Woh cheez seekhne mein saal lagte hain jo bahut kam log kar sakte hain. Meera ke saalon ka koi shortcut nahi tha.
>
> **Leverage** beech mein hai, aur uski shakal alag hai. Woh dheere-dheere nahi badhta. Woh ek din chalu hota hai. Anil ki naali do saal tak zero thi, phir ek din poori. Isliye leverage sabse zyada chhoda jaata hai: uska bada hissa aisa dikhta hai jaise kuch ho hi nahi raha.

**3. (jodne wala)** Pichhle chapter mein paisa "value dene ka record" tha. Anil ko do saal baad bhi paani ka hissa milta rehta hai. Woh record kis cheez ka hai, aur woh value di kab gayi thi?

> **Jawab:** Woh record us naali ka hai, na ki aaj ke kisi kaam ka. Value do saal pehle di gayi thi, jab woh khod raha tha.
>
> Ravi roz value deta hai aur roz record kamata hai. Anil ne do saal ek saath value di aur aisa record kamaya jo baar baar kaam aata hai.
>
> Toh gaon Anil ko "kuch na karne" ke paise nahi de raha. Woh us kaam ke paise de raha hai jo poora ho chuka hai aur ab bhi kaam kar raha hai. Yeh Chapter 0.1 wale rule ka seedha nateeja hai: value di gayi thi, isliye tokens aa rahe hain.
>
> Yahin par leverage ki asli definition milti hai: **aisi value dena jo ek baar di jaaye aur baar baar mile.**

---

# Chapter 0.3  [SPINE]
## Society ke chaar levels

---
### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Gaon badh gaya. Sau log ab ek crore hain. Valley sheher ban gayi. Ek cheez toot gayi jiske baare mein kisi ne socha hi nahi tha. Batao kya?

**Kabir:** Bheed ho gayi?

**Madhav:** Usse gehri baat. Gaon mein tumhe pata tha ki Meera haddi jodti hai. Uske ghar tak chal ke ja sakte the. Ab ek crore logon mein tumhe kitne log pata hain?

**Kabir:** Do sau, teen sau.

**Madhav:** Toh aaj subah tumne jo chawal khaya, woh kisne ugaya?

**Kabir:** Pata nahi.

**Madhav:** Paani kis pipe se aaya, kisne daali?

**Kabir:** Pata nahi.

**Madhav:** Wahi kaam abhi bhi ho raha hai. Roz. Lekin purana arrangement ise organise nahi kar sakta, kyunki purana arrangement ek doosre ko jaanne pe khada tha. Toh ab yeh chalta kaise hai? Sabse neeche se shuru karo. Ek insaan ko chahiye kya?

**Kabir:** Khaana, paani, ghar, ilaaj, suraksha, padhai. Aur kisi ka saath.

**Madhav:** Aur? Ek cheez chhoot rahi hai.

**Kabir:** Kuch aisa jisse din bitane laayak lagein.

**Madhav:** Haan. Ab batao, yeh list gaon wale ke liye alag thi?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Bilkul yehi thi.

**Madhav:** Paanch hazaar saal pehle?

**Kabir:** Wahi.

**Madhav:** Toh yeh list hilti nahi. Sab kuch iske upar khada hai. Ab agla kadam. Meera sau logon ko sambhal leti thi. Ek crore ko khaana kaun khilayega?

**Kabir:** Ek aadmi toh nahi kar sakta.

**Madhav:** Toh?

**Kabir:** Log mil kar karenge. Ek group.

**Madhav:** Aur woh group kis kis ko khilayega?

**Kabir:** Un logon ko jinhe woh jaanta bhi nahi.

**Madhav:** Bas, yahi scale ka jawab hai. Ek aadmi unhe serve karta hai jinhe woh jaanta hai. Ek group unhe serve karta hai jinhe usmein se koi nahi jaanta. Ab teesra kadam. Har group ek hi sawal poochta hai. Kya?

**Kabir:** Zyada paisa kaise kamayein?

**Madhav:** Usse pehle wala sawal. Woh zyada logon tak kaise pahunchein, bina zyada aadmi lagaye?

**Kabir:** Koi machine. Koi auzaar.

**Madhav:** Hal. Pahiya. Likha hua hisaab. Telephone. Ab dhyaan se batao: hal kitne logon ko khaana khilata hai?

**Kabir:** Sabko. Usise toh kheti hoti hai.

**Madhav:** Nahi. Hal ne aaj tak ek bhi aadmi ko khaana nahi diya. Woh kisi ke muh mein kuch nahi daalta.

**Kabir:** Toh phir woh karta kya hai?

**Madhav:** Kisan ko guna karta hai. Aur kisan logon ko khilata hai.

**Kabir:** Haan. Auzaar seedha insaan ko serve nahi karta. Woh us cheez ko badata hai jo insaan ko serve kar rahi hai.

**Madhav:** Ise yaad rakhna, agle chapter ka poora chapter isi pe hai. Ab aakhri kadam. Ab tak har auzaar ek hi kaam karta tha. Hal hal chalata hai. Use mana kar hisaab-kitaab nahi karwa sakte. Sahi?

**Kabir:** Sahi.

**Madhav:** Aur aaj?

**Kabir:** Aaj aisi cheez hai jo hisaab bhi likhti hai, message bhi, scan bhi padhti hai, raasta bhi banati hai.

**Madhav:** Toh woh ek kaam ka auzaar hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Woh... auzaar banane ka auzaar hai.

**Madhav:** Ab poora dhaancha bolo. Neeche se upar.

**Kabir:** Insaan ki zaroorat. Uske upar group jo use scale pe poora karta hai. Uske upar auzaar jo group ko guna karta hai. Aur uske upar woh cheez jo auzaaron ko behtar karti hai.

**Madhav:** Chaar levels. Aur aakhri sawal: paisa in mein kahan ghusta hai?

**Kabir:** Sabse upar? AI companies ke paas toh bahut paisa hai.

**Madhav:** Ghusta kahan hai, rukta kahan hai, yeh alag sawal hai. Jab tum khaana khareedte ho, tumhare haath se paisa kis level pe nikalta hai?

**Kabir:** Level ek pe. Meri zaroorat pe.

**Madhav:** Bas. Paisa hamesha sabse neeche se ghusta hai, jab ek insaan apni zaroorat ke liye deta hai. Uske baad woh upar chadhta hai. Upar wale levels paisa banate nahi, woh neeche se aaya hua paisa aapas mein baantte hain.

---

### Naam

Chaar levels. Har ek apne neeche wale ko serve karne ke liye hai.

```
    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  4   ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE             │   auzaaron ko behtar karta hai
    └───────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
                        ↓ serve karta hai
    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  3   TECHNOLOGY                          │   group ko guna karta hai
    └───────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
                        ↓ serve karta hai
    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  2   ORGANISATIONS (business)            │   ajnabiyon ko scale pe serve
    └───────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
                        ↓ serve karta hai
    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  1   HUMAN NEEDS                         │   sab kuch isi wajah se hai
    └──────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Service NEECHE ki taraf behti hai.
    Paisa UPAR ki taraf, aur woh sirf level 1 pe hi ghusta hai,
    jab ek insaan apni zaroorat ke liye paise deta hai.
```

Teen cheezein check karne laayak hain, kyunki saaf-suthra diagram inhe chhupa deta hai.

**Har zaroorat business se poori nahi hoti.** Maa apne bachche ko khila rahi hai, yeh level 1 seedha serve ho raha hai, na koi organisation, na paisa kahin. Insaan ko ek doosre se jo chahiye, uska bahut bada hissa kabhi transaction tha hi nahi aur kabhi hoga bhi nahi.

**Har business ko technology se madad nahi milti.** Kuch ko mushkil se chhoti hai. Kuch jaan-boojh ke mana karte hain, aur wahi mana karna hi unka product hai.

**Har technology ko AI behtar nahi karta.** Paani ki pipe technology hai. Usmein sochne wali machine ke liye karne ko kuch hai hi nahi.

Yeh stack ek jhukav batata hai, kanoon nahi. Jahan levels gayab hain, aksar wahi jagah sabse interesting hoti hai.

---

### Asli mein

Koi bhi zaroorat uthao, uske upar chaaron levels abhi baithe milenge.

```
ZAROORAT      LEVEL 2            LEVEL 3           LEVEL 4
khaana    →   restaurant,    →   fridge,       →   demand ka andaaza
              farm               tractor

sehat     →   hospital       →   scanner,      →   scan padhna
                                 dawaiyan

gyaan     →   school,        →   chhapai,      →   har sawal ka jawab
              university         video             dene wala tutor

suraksha  →   police,        →   radio,        →   pattern pakadna
              insurance          camera

judav     →   post, telecom  →   phone,        →   kisi bhi do bhaashaon
                                 internet          ke beech tarjuma
```

Har column bhara hua hai. Us table ka har level 4 wala entry aapse chhota hai.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

Aaj raat aap phone se khaana order karte ho. Chaaron levels ek saath chal rahe hain, aur aap sirf ek button dekhte ho.

```
LEVEL 1   Bhookh. Bas. Yeh cheez badalti hi nahi.

LEVEL 2   Ek company jo aapko un rasoiyon se jodti hai jinse aap
          kabhi mile nahi. Restaurant khaana banata hai, delivery
          wala laata hai, dono aapke liye ajnabi hain.
          Yeh woh "group" hai jo ajnabiyon ko scale pe serve karta hai.

LEVEL 3   Phone, internet, GPS, map, payment system.
          Inmein se koi bhi aapko khaana nahi khilata.
          Yeh sab us company ko guna karte hain.
          GPS ke bina ek delivery wala ek din mein 4 order kar paata.
          GPS ke saath 25.

LEVEL 4   Kitne order kahan se aayenge iska andaaza.
          Kaunsa raasta chuna jaaye. Kaunsa delivery wala kaunse
          order ke liye. Yeh level 3 ke auzaaron ko behtar karta hai.
```

Ab neeche se upar ginti karo. Level 1 hazaaron saal purana hai. Level 2 ki shakal purani hai, dhaabe hamesha the. Level 3 ke tukde 20 se 40 saal purane hain. Level 4 lagbhag 10 saal purana hai.

Ek order, chaar levels, aur unki umron mein hazaaron saal ka farak.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh maan lena ki upar wale level pe hone se apne aap zyada paisa milta hai.**

"AI mein chala jaaunga" sunne mein aisa lagta hai jaise woh khud ek jawab hai. Woh nahi hai. Woh sirf batata hai ki aap kaunsi manzil pe khade honge, na ki us manzil pe aapka kya haal hoga.

Level 4 pe hazaaron log naukri karte hain, ghante bechte hain, aur unka leverage dial bilkul zero pe hai. Woh bas Ravi hain, ek nayi manzil pe. Udhar Level 1 pe koi kisan hai jiske paas apni zameen hai aur woh Ravi se kai guna kama raha hai.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai kyunki level dikhta hai aur position nahi. Level ka naam hota hai jo aap kisi ko bata sakte ho. Position ka koi naam nahi hota.

Do maps ko saath rakhna hi asli kaam hai: **level batata hai ki aap kis kaam ke aas-paas ho, dials batate hain ki us kaam se aapko kya milega.**

---

### Map pe

Ab aapke paas do map hain jo aapas mein fit hote hain.

Pehla map, pichhle chapter se, batata hai ki **kisi ko kitna milega yeh kya tay karta hai**: value, scarcity aur leverage.

Yeh map batata hai ki **society mein saara kaam hota kahan hai**: chaar levels, har ek apne neeche wale ko serve karta hua.

Dono ko saath rakho aur ek cheez dikhti hai: har level pe aise log hain jo teeno dials pe neeche hain, aur aise bhi jo teeno pe upar hain. Koi level apne aap ameer nahi hai. Sirf levels ke andar ki positions hoti hain.

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Pichhle hafte aapne kisi cheez ke paise diye. Use neeche ki taraf trace karo: kaunsi organisation ne di, woh organisation kis technology pe depend karti hai, aur woh aakhir mein us list mein se kaunsi zaroorat poori karti hai? Agar aap list tak pahunch hi nahi paate, toh woh us cheez ke baare mein kya batata hai jo aapne khareedi?

> **Jawab:** Zyadatar cheezein 2 ya 3 kadmon mein list tak pahunch jaati hain. Chai → dukaan → supply chain → khaana. Recharge → telecom company → tower aur fibre → judav.
>
> Agar aap list tak nahi pahunch paate, toh do mein se ek baat hai.
>
> **Ek:** woh cheez kisi aur ki zaroorat poori karti hai, aapki nahi. Business ke liye khareedi gayi cheezein aksar aisi hoti hain. Woh list tak pahunchti hain, bas kisi aur ke raaste se.
>
> **Do:** woh zaroorat ki nahi, chaah ki cheez hai. Yeh galat nahi hai, insaan hamesha aisi cheezein khareedte aaye hain. Lekin dhyaan rakho: zaroorat ki cheezein mandi mein tikti hain aur chaah ki cheezein sabse pehle katti hain.

**2. (jodne wala)** Chapter 0.2 ke teen dials yaad karo. Kya level 4 pe hone se apne aap leverage mil jaata hai? Ek aisa aadmi socho jo level 4 pe kaam karta hai aur jiska leverage phir bhi kam hai.

> **Jawab:** Nahi, bilkul nahi milta.
>
> Ek aadmi jo kisi company mein AI ka kaam karta hai aur ghante ke hisaab se tankhwah leta hai, uska leverage utna hi hai jitna Ravi ka. Woh chhutti pe jaaye toh uski kamai rukti hai. Kaam woh kar raha hai, lekin jo cheez baar baar chalti rahegi uska maalik koi aur hai.
>
> **Leverage level se nahi aata. Woh iss baat se aata hai ki jo cheez aapke bina chalti rahegi, uspe aapka haq hai ya nahi.**
>
> Isi tarah, level 1 pe bhi bahut leverage ho sakta hai. Jis kisan ke paas apni zameen hai, uske paas ek aisi cheez hai jo har saal fasal deti hai. Zameen uski naali hai.

**3. (design sawal, iss part ka)** Ek zaroorat chuno jo aapko lagta hai theek se poori nahi hoti. Uske chaaron levels likho jaisi woh aaj hain. Phir woh jagah nishaan lagao jahan koi level gayab hai ya kamzor hai. Yeh `WORKBOOK.md` mein likho.

> **Jawab:** Iska ek sahi jawab nahi hai, kyunki yeh aapka apna kaam hai. Lekin dekhne ke liye do cheezein batata hoon.
>
> **Gayab level 3:** ek business chal raha hai lekin usko guna karne wala koi auzaar nahi hai. Log haath se register bhar rahe hain, phone pe hisaab kar rahe hain. Yeh sabse aam khaali jagah hai aur sabse aasan bharne wali.
>
> **Gayab level 2:** zaroorat asli hai lekin use scale pe serve karne wala koi group hi nahi hai. Yeh dhoondhna mushkil hai aur milne pe bada hota hai.
>
> Ek chetavni: khaali jagah dikhne ka matlab hamesha mauka nahi hota. Kabhi kabhi level isliye khaali hai kyunki wahan paisa nahi hai, ya kisi ne koshish karke chhod diya. Chapter 8.5 mein hum isi pe wapas aayenge.

---

# Chapter 0.4  [SPINE]
## Technology level dikhta kyun nahi

---
### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Aaj subah nashte se pehle tumne kitni technologies istemaal ki?

**Kabir:** Do teen. Phone, light.

**Madhav:** Ginti karo. Switch dabaya, roshni aayi. Nal khola, saaf paani aaya. Phone uthaya, kisi se baat ki jo sheher se bahar tha. Message bheja jo second se kam mein pahuncha. Fridge se doodh nikaala jo garmi mein bhi kharaab nahi tha.

**Kabir:** Theek hai, das ke aas-paas.

**Madhav:** In dus mein se kitni tum kisi ko samjha sakte ho?

**Kabir:** Sach kahun toh ek bhi nahi.

**Madhav:** Aur tumhe iss baat ki sharam aati hai.

**Kabir:** Haan. Lagta hai baaki sabko pata hai.

**Madhav:** Toh chalo dekhte hain kiski galti hai. Ek nal ka kaam kya hai?

**Kabir:** Paani dena.

**Madhav:** Bas itna?

**Kabir:** Haan.

**Madhav:** Ab socho. Agar nal aisa hota ki har baar tumhe yaad rakhna padta ki paani kis bandh se aa raha hai, kaunsa pump use upar bhej raha hai, kaunsi pipe kis gali se guzarti hai, kaunse kemikal se woh saaf hua. Woh nal kaisa hota?

**Kabir:** Bahut bura. Kaam hi nahi karta.

**Madhav:** Toh achha nal woh hai jo tumhe yeh sab bhoolne deta hai.

**Kabir:** Haan.

**Madhav:** Ab isko poore technology pe lagao. Gaadi tumse kya chhupa rahi hai?

**Kabir:** Engine. Gearbox. Tel kahan se aaya.

**Madhav:** Switch?

**Kabir:** Power plant. Grid. Taar.

**Madhav:** Search box?

**Kabir:** Poori duniya ke computer.

**Madhav:** Toh ek technology ka kaam kya hai?

**Kabir:** Apne neeche wali cheez ko chhupa dena.

**Madhav:** Ab yeh bolo, aur dhyaan se bolo. Ek technology jitni achhi hoti hai, tum usse utna...

**Kabir:** Utna zyada samajhte ho.

**Madhav:** Ulta.

**Kabir:** Utna kam samajhte ho?

**Madhav:** Bolo phir se, poora.

**Kabir:** Technology jitni achhi hogi, main usse utna kam samjhunga. Kyunki achhi hone ka matlab hi hai ki woh gayab ho jaaye.

**Madhav:** Ab batao, tumhe grid ke baare mein kab pata chalta hai?

**Kabir:** Jab light chali jaati hai.

**Madhav:** Internet ke baare mein?

**Kabir:** Jab band ho jaata hai.

**Madhav:** Toh tum ek aisi duniya mein rehte ho jahan sab kuch theek chal raha hai, aur isiliye kuch samajh nahi aata. Ab wapas apni sharam pe aao. Kiski galti hai?

**Kabir:** Kisi ki nahi. Cheezein banai hi aisi gayi hain.

**Madhav:** Sahi. Lekin ab main tumse ek aur cheez poochunga, aur iska jawab aasan nahi hai. Jo cheez chhupi hui hai, uske saath tum kya kar sakte ho?

**Kabir:** Use istemaal kar sakta hoon.

**Madhav:** Aur kya nahi kar sakte?

**Kabir:** Main... use chun nahi sakta. Do cheezon mein se behtar nahi chun sakta, kyunki mujhe farak hi nahi pata.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Main uske upar kuch bana nahi sakta.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Main yeh nahi keh sakta ki uske saath kya mumkin hai aur kya nahi.

**Madhav:** Bas. Toh galti tumhari nahi hai, aur keemat phir bhi tum hi chukate ho. Yeh do baatein ek saath sach hain, aur zyadatar log inmein se ek chun lete hain aur doosri bhool jaate hain.

---

### Naam

Neeche wali cheez ko chhupa dene ke iss tareeke ka naam hai **abstraction**.

Yeh technology ka sabse zaroori aur sabse kam samjha jaane wala idea hai. Poori computer ki duniya iske alaawa lagbhag kuch bhi nahi hai: parton ka dher, jahan har parat neeche wali ko chhupa deti hai, taaki uske upar wala usse soche bina kaam kar sake.

```
aap ek app istemaal karte ho     taaki program ke baare mein na sochna pade
program ek language istemaal     taaki 1 aur 0 ke baare mein na sochna pade
language ek OS istemaal karti    taaki chip ke baare mein na sochna pade
OS chip istemaal karta hai       taaki electron ke baare mein na sochna pade
```

Koi bhi saari parton ko gehrai se nahi jaanta. Sab kisi chhupi hui parat pe khade hain. Yeh aapke gyaan ka gaddha nahi hai. Yeh poora design hai.

---

### Asli mein

Jab bijli pehli baar sheheron mein aayi, log ise samajhte the. Ghar ke malik apna fuse badalte the aur unhe pata hota tha ki circuit kaise banta hai. Woh isliye nahi ki woh aaj ke logon se hoshiyaar the. Woh isliye ki technology abhi itni achhi nahi thi ki gayab ho sake.

Ab bijli aapke liye ek switch hai. Behtar technology, kam samajh. Yeh exchange har ek cheez ke saath hua hai.

Aur wahi abhi AI ke saath ho raha hai, sirf bahut tezi se. Aap ek box mein type karte ho aur jawab aa jaata hai. Beech ka sab kuch pehle hi gayab ho chuka hai, jabki cheez bani hue paanch saal bhi theek se nahi hue.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

1958 mein Leonard Read ne ek chhota lekh likha jiska naam tha "I, Pencil". Usmein ek pencil apni kahani khud sunati hai.

Uski baat ek hi thi: **duniya mein ek bhi aisa insaan nahi hai jo ek pencil bana sake.**

Lakdi kisi ne kaati, aari kisi aur ne banayi, us aari ka steel kisi teesre ne, us steel ka loha kisi khadaan se. Graphite Sri Lanka se, mitti Mississippi se, mom, rang, rubber, us rubber ko jodne wala kemikal. Us pencil ke peeche hazaaron log hain, alag deshon mein, jo ek doosre ko jaante tak nahi, aur jinmein se koi bhi akela pencil nahi bana sakta.

Ab yeh yaad rakho aur socho ki ek phone kya cheez hai. Ya ek AI model.

Aur ab dhyaan do ki iss baat ne aapko chaunkaya. **Pencil sabse simple cheez hai jo aap soch sakte ho.** Woh bhi chhupi hui hai. Yeh soch ki "mujhe technology samajh nahi aati" us pencil se hi shuru ho jaati hai.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki technology samajh na aana meri kami hai.**

Yeh baat andar hi andar chalti rehti hai. Log sochte hain ki baaki sabko yeh sab pata hai aur woh akele peeche reh gaye. Isse sharm aati hai, aur sharm ki wajah se log sawal poochna band kar dete hain, jisse gaddha aur bada hota hai.

Lekin jo abhi aapne dekha, uske hisaab se yeh galat hai. Cheezein banai hi is tarah gayi hain ki samjhe bina chal jaayein. **Aapko woh nahi dikh raha jo dikhane ke liye banaya hi nahi gaya tha.**

Ab yahan par ek baat saaf kar deta hoon, kyunki iska ulta bhi galat hai.

Yeh iss baat ka bahana nahi hai ki samajhne ki koshish hi na ki jaaye. Pichhla section abhi bata chuka hai ki chhupi hui cheez ko aap istemaal toh kar sakte ho, lekin uspe kuch bana nahi sakte. Toh kami aapki nahi hai, aur keemat phir bhi aapko hi chukani padti hai.

Sahi baat yeh hai: **peeche hona aapki galti nahi hai, lekin usse nikalna aapka hi kaam hai.**

---

### Map pe

```
    JO AAP DEKHTE HO
    ┌──────────────────────────┐
    │   nal, switch, app       │
    └──────────┬───────────────┘
               │  abstraction ki deewar
    ═══════════╪══════════════════════════════
               │
    ┌──────────┴───────────────┐
    │   bandh, grid, server    │   JO CHHUPA HAI
    │   pipe, taar, network    │
    │   chip, electron         │
    └──────────────────────────┘

    Deewar jaan-boojh ke banayi gayi hai.
    Yeh kitaab usmein ek darwaza banane ke liye hai.
```

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Aaj aapne jo cheezein istemaal ki hain, unmein se ek chuno, aur us sabki list banao jo woh aapse chhupa rahi hai. Kahan tak neeche ja sakte ho, jab tak aapko sach mein pata na ho ki neeche kya hai?

> **Jawab:** Zyadatar log 2 ya 3 kadam neeche jaakar ruk jaate hain. Nal se pipe tak, pipe se tanki tak, aur uske baad andhera.
>
> Woh rukne ki jagah hi kaam ki cheez hai. Woh batati hai ki aapki abstraction ki deewar kahan khadi hai.
>
> Aur dhyaan do ki har cheez ke liye woh deewar alag jagah hai. Jo cheez aapne khud thodi bahut kholi hai, uske liye aap gehre ja paate ho. Yeh saabit karta hai ki deewar aapke dimaag ki seema nahi hai. Woh sirf yeh hai ki aapne ab tak kya khola aur kya nahi.

**2. (samajh check)** Chapter kehta hai ki achhi technology gayab ho jaati hai. Toh phir ek achhi technology aur ek aisi cheez mein kya farak hai jo hai hi nahi? Aap doosron ko kaise samjhaoge ki koi chhupi hui cheez wahan maujood hai?

> **Jawab:** Farak tab dikhta hai jab woh **ruk jaaye**.
>
> Jo cheez hai hi nahi, woh ruk nahi sakti. Achhi technology jab rukti hai toh poora ka poora andar ka jaal ek saath saamne aa jaata hai. Light jaane ke din aapko pata chalta hai ki bijli ka grid maujood tha.
>
> Isiliye zyadatar log technology ke baare mein sirf uske toot-ne se seekhte hain. Aur isiliye woh seekh tukdon mein rehti hai: aapko sirf woh hissa dikhta hai jo us din toota tha.
>
> Ek doosra tareeka bhi hai: keemat dekho. Har chhupi hui cheez ko koi na koi chala raha hai aur uska kharcha uth raha hai. Jahan paisa ja raha hai, wahan kuch maujood hai. Part 7 mein hum yehi karenge.

**3. (jodne wala)** Chapter 0.2 kehta tha ki pay value, scarcity aur leverage se aata hai. Agar abstraction ka matlab hai ki bahut kam log neeche wali parat samajhte hain, toh us parat ko samajhne wale ke liye scarcity ka dial kahan baithta hai? Aur woh dial waqt ke saath kis taraf khisak raha hai?

> **Jawab:** Jitni gehri parat, utna upar scarcity ka dial. Yeh seedha nikal aata hai: samajhne wale kam hain, isliye jo samajhta hai woh durlabh hai.
>
> Dooosra hissa zyada important hai, aur uska jawab do tarah se sach hai.
>
> **Ek taraf:** har naya abstraction ek purani parat ko scarce se common bana deta hai. Pehle server chalane ke liye machine ka gehra gyaan chahiye tha. Cloud aane ke baad kam log ko uski zaroorat rahi. Us parat ka dial neeche khisak gaya.
>
> **Doosri taraf:** wahi abstraction ek nayi, aur upar wali parat bana deta hai, jahan dial ekdum upar hai, kyunki cheez nayi hai aur abhi kisi ko samajh nahi aayi.
>
> Toh scarcity gayab nahi hoti. Woh upar khisak jaati hai.
>
> Yeh iss poori kitaab ka sabse kaam ka nateeja hai, aur Book 2 isi jagah pe khadi hai. AI abhi wahi nayi parat hai.

---

# Chapter 0.5  [SPINE]
## Toh ab hume kya kholna padega

---
### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ab tak tumhare paas do cheezein hain. Ek naksha, jo batata hai ki society ka kaam kahan hota hai. Aur ek formula, jo batata hai ki kisi ko kitna milega. Ab in dono ko saath rakho aur mujhe batao ki tumhe seekhna kya chahiye.

**Kabir:** Level chaar. AI.

**Madhav:** Kyun?

**Kabir:** Kyunki wahi sabse upar hai.

**Madhav:** Sabse upar hone se kya milta hai?

**Kabir:** Zyada... paisa?

**Madhav:** Pichhle chapter mein hi humne dekha ki level se kuch nahi milta. Dobara socho. Formula ke teen dials lo, ek-ek karke. Pehla, problem ka size. Woh kis level pe tay hota hai?

**Kabir:** Level ek pe. Insaan ki zaroorat pe.

**Madhav:** Aur woh list badalti hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Paanch hazaar saal se wahi hai.

**Madhav:** Toh us dial ko samajhne ke liye tumhe kya seekhna padega?

**Kabir:** Kuch bhi nahi. Main khud insaan hoon, mujhe pata hai logon ko kya chahiye.

**Madhav:** Bas. Pehla dial khula hua hai. Ab doosra, scarcity. Woh kis cheez pe khada hai?

**Kabir:** Iss baat pe ki kitne log woh kaam kar sakte hain.

**Madhav:** Aur kaunsa level aisa hai jise sabse kam log samajhte hain?

**Kabir:** Level teen. Jo chhupa hua hai.

**Madhav:** Toh scarcity ka dial apne aap kahan sabse upar hoga?

**Kabir:** Wahin, jahan chhupa hua hai.

**Madhav:** Ab teesra. Leverage. Anil ki naali. Aaj ke zamane mein woh "naali" kya hai?

**Kabir:** Software. Ya koi system. Koi machine.

**Madhav:** Woh kis level pe baithte hain?

**Kabir:** Teen pe. Phir se teen pe.

**Madhav:** Toh teeno dials ek saath kahan ishaara kar rahe hain?

**Kabir:** Ek dial khula hai, aur baaki do ek hi chhupi hui parat ke peeche hain.

**Madhav:** Bas. Yeh poori kitaab ka tark hai, ek line mein. Ab ek chetavni, kyunki tum abhi ek galat nateeja nikalne wale ho. Kya main yeh keh raha hoon ki level teen pe jaane se tum ameer ho jaoge?

**Kabir:** Nahi... kyunki har level pe neeche wale log bhi hain.

**Madhav:** Sahi. Main sirf itna keh raha hoon ki jo do dials tumhare haath mein hain, dono ek hi darwaze ke peeche hain. Darwaza kholna zaroori hai. Kaafi nahi hai. Ab aakhri sawal, aur yeh sabse practical hai. Us parat ko samajhne ke do tareeke hain. Batao.

**Kabir:** Ek toh yeh ki jo cheez saamne aaye woh padh lo. Aaj database, kal cloud.

**Madhav:** Tum abhi tak yehi kar rahe the.

**Kabir:** Haan.

**Madhav:** Aur nateeja?

**Kabir:** Har hafte kuch naya sunta hoon jo baaki sabko pehle se pata tha.

**Madhav:** Kyun? Tum sust toh nahi ho.

**Kabir:** Kyunki har cheez alag se aati hai. Uske liye mere paas koi jagah nahi hoti.

**Madhav:** Aur jab jagah na ho toh har nayi cheez kya lagti hai?

**Kabir:** Khabar. Aur khabar hamesha der se aati hai.

**Madhav:** Toh doosra tareeka kya hoga?

**Kabir:** Neeche se poora dhaancha banana. Pehle jagah banao, phir cheezein apne aap baith jaayengi.

**Madhav:** Woh dheema lagta hai.

**Kabir:** Shuru mein.

**Madhav:** Aur baad mein?

**Kabir:** Baad mein... har agli cheez sasti ho jaati hai. Kyunki mujhe yaad nahi karni padti, sirf jagah dhoondhni padti hai.

**Madhav:** Toh hum kahan se shuru karenge?

**Kabir:** Sabse neeche se. Jo meri zindagi se sabse door cheez lagti hai.

**Madhav:** Ek switch se. Chaar hafte lagenge. Uske baad AI tumhe ek alag duniya nahi, ek aur chapter lagega.

---

### Naam

Iss kitaab ka baaki hissa woh doosra tareeka hai, poora ka poora.

Aur order ulta lagega, toh yeh saaf keh deta hoon. Hum sabse neeche se shuru karenge, ek switch se, jo aapki rozmarra ki zindagi se sabse door cheez lagti hai.

Woh isliye ki har parat sirf tabhi samajh aati hai jab uske neeche wali parat pehle se khadi ho. Beech se shuru karoge toh udhaar lena padega, aur udhaar liya hua gyaan hi woh gyaan hai jo tikta nahi.

Chaar hafte lagenge. Uske baad Book 2 mein jo AI hai, woh khud ba khud ek chapter jaisa lagega, na ki ek alag duniya.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

Jab koi nayi programming language ya naya framework aata hai, ek cheez baar baar dikhti hai.

Naya seekhne wala usmein mahine lagata hai. Purana kaam karne wala usmein ek hafta lagata hai.

Wajah yeh nahi hai ki woh tez hai. Wajah yeh hai ki uske paas dhaancha pehle se hai. Jab woh nayi cheez padhta hai, toh use naye ideas nahi mil rahe. Use yeh mil raha hai ki jo ideas woh pehle se jaanta hai, unhe iss baar kis naam se bulaya gaya hai aur kaunse do-teen faisle alag liye gaye hain.

Woh yaad nahi kar raha. Woh sirf farak note kar raha hai.

Yehi cheez har layer pe kaam karti hai. Ek baar aap samajh gaye ki data store kaise hota hai, toh har naya database ek naya subject nahi rehta. Woh ek jaana-pehchana subject ban jaata hai jismein kuch alag chunav kiye gaye hain.

Yeh iss kitaab ka poora vaada hai. Aap 112 cheezein yaad nahi karoge. Aap ek dhaancha banaoge, aur uske baad nayi cheezein yaad karne ki zaroorat hi nahi padegi.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki neeche se shuru karna dheema raasta hai.**

Aisa lagta hai ki seedha kaam ki cheez pe jaana tez hoga. AI seekhna hai toh AI padho, switch se kya matlab.

Yeh pehle do hafte tak sach lagta hai, aur uske baad ulta ho jaata hai. Kyunki jo aadmi tukdon mein seekh raha hai, use har nayi cheez zero se seekhni padti hai. Jo aadmi dhaancha bana raha hai, uski har agli cheez pichhli se sasti hoti jaati hai.

Ek saal baad tukde wale ke paas sau alag cheezein hoti hain, jinmein se aadhi bhool chuki hoti hain. Dhaanche wale ke paas ek cheez hoti hai jismein sau jagah hain.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai ki tukdon mein seekhne mein **shuruaat mein zyada tez lagta hai**. Aapko roz kuch naya pata chalta hai. Dhaancha banane mein shuruaat mein kuch bhi kaam ka nahi milta. Ravi aur Anil, phir se, ab seekhne mein.

### Ek cheez jo saath chalegi

Iss kitaab mein hum ek chhote se product ko saath lekar chalenge:

**Ek chat app, jo aapke saare messages sambhal ke rakhta hai, aur unmein se kuch bhi dhoondh sakta hai.**

Bas itna. Aisa lagta hai ki ek aadmi ek hafte mein bana le.

Har part ke aakhir mein hum us app pe wapas aayenge. Aur har baar pata chalega ki uske andar ek aur parat chhupi thi jo pichhli baar dikhi hi nahi thi.

Kitaab ke aakhir tak aap us app ko poora khol chuke honge, upar se neeche tak, aur us waqt aapko yeh bhi pata hoga ki use banane mein kitna waqt lagta.

---

---

### Part 0 ka gate

Aage badhne se pehle in teen sawalon ka jawab do. Agar nahi aata, toh bataye gaye chapter pe wapas jao.

**1.** Ek aadmi hafte mein saath ghante kaam karta hai aur bahut kam kamata hai. Uski kamai badhane ke teen alag raaste batao, aur har raaste ke liye batao ki kaunsa dial ghum raha hai. *(nahi aaya toh 0.2)*

> **Jawab:** Teen raaste, teen dials.
>
> **Badi problem pe jaana.** Wahi hunar, lekin aisi jagah lagana jahan na hone ka nuksaan bada ho. Ek hi kaam karne wala aadmi kisi aur jagah kai guna kama sakta hai, sirf isliye ki wahan uska rukna zyada mehnga padta hai. Dial: size.
>
> **Aisa kuch seekhna jo kam log kar sakte hain.** Dheema, saalon ka kaam. Dial: scarcity.
>
> **Aisi cheez banana jo uske bina chale.** Ek auzaar, ek system, ek likhi hui cheez, ek business jismein doosre log kaam karein. Dial: leverage.
>
> Ek chauthi cheez bhi log aksar kehte hain: aur zyada ghante. Woh dial hai hi nahi, aur uski ek deewar hai. Chapter 0.2 ka pehla sawal wahi tha.

**2.** Level 3 ki koi cheez uthao, aur teen level 2 wale kaam batao jinhe woh guna karti hai. Phir batao ki woh level 1 ki kaunsi zaroorat tak pahunchti hai. *(nahi aaya toh 0.3)*

> **Jawab:** Ek udaharan: GPS.
>
> Woh delivery ko guna karta hai (ek banda 4 ki jagah 25 order kar leta hai), taxi ko guna karta hai (bina raasta poochhe kahin bhi), aur samaan bhejne wali company ko guna karta hai (poora truck kahan hai, hamesha pata).
>
> Level 1 tak: khaana, judav, aur samaan ka pahunchna.
>
> Sahi jawab kai hain. Check yeh hai ki aapne teeno kaamon ke liye wahi ek auzaar rakha, aur teeno alag business the. Jo cheez sirf ek hi business ko guna karti hai, woh aksar level 3 nahi, us business ka ek hissa hoti hai.

**3.** Ek achhi technology aur ek buri technology mein, aapko kaunsi zyada samajh aati hai, aur aisa kyun hai? *(nahi aaya toh 0.4)*

> **Jawab:** Buri wali.
>
> Buri technology aapko andar ka hissa dikhati hai kyunki woh use chhupa nahi paati. Woh atakti hai, error deti hai, aur aapko andar jaakar theek karna padta hai. Us process mein aapko pata chal jaata hai ki andar kya hai.
>
> Achhi technology kuch nahi dikhati. Woh bas chal jaati hai.
>
> Isiliye purane zamane ke log apni cheezein zyada samajhte the. Woh hoshiyaar nahi the. Unki cheezein zyada kharaab thin.

---

**Part 0 aur paise ka rishta:** yeh poora part hi paise ke baare mein tha. Aage ke parts machine ke baare mein hain. Woh dhaaga Part 7 ke aakhir mein wapas judega, jab hum poochenge ki iss sab ko chalane ka kharcha kaun uthata hai.

# Chapter 1.1  [SPINE]
## Ek switch dabane se kya hota hai

---

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Deewar pe jo switch hai, use dabao.

**Kabir:** Dabaya. Light jal gayi.

**Madhav:** Ab batao, tumne kiya kya?

**Kabir:** Switch dabaya.

**Madhav:** Switch ne kya kiya?

**Kabir:** Light chalu ki.

**Madhav:** Nahi. Switch ne light ko chhua bhi nahi. Woh deewar mein hai, light chhat pe hai. Switch ne kya kiya?

**Kabir:** Bijli jaane di.

**Madhav:** Kaise?

**Kabir:** Taar jud gaya. Pehle raasta toota hua tha, ab jud gaya.

**Madhav:** Bas. Ek switch sirf ek kaam karta hai: raasta jodta hai ya todta hai. Aur uski kitni haalatein hain?

**Kabir:** Do. Juda hua, ya toota hua.

**Madhav:** Sirf do? Aadha juda hua nahi ho sakta?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Ya toh bijli jaa rahi hai ya nahi jaa rahi.

**Madhav:** Ise likhne ka ek tareeka batao. Sabse chhota tareeka.

**Kabir:** Haan ya na. Chalu ya band.

**Madhav:** Aur agar sirf do nishaan istemaal karne hon?

**Kabir:** 1 aur 0.

**Madhav:** Theek hai. Ab ek sawal poochta hoon jo ajeeb lagega. Tumhare ghar mein kitne switch hain?

**Kabir:** Pandrah, bees.

**Madhav:** Aur tumhare phone ke andar?

**Kabir:** Phone mein switch kahan hote hain?

**Madhav:** Poocha maine. Andaza lagao.

**Kabir:** Kuch nahi honge. Woh toh chip hai.

**Madhav:** Ek aam phone ki chip mein aaj kuch **arab** switch hote hain. Itne chhote ki ek baal ki mothai mein hazaaron aa jaayein. Lekin kaam wahi karte hain jo deewar wala karta hai. Raasta jodo ya todo.

**Kabir:** Arab switch se kya hoga? Woh toh sirf light jalate hain.

**Madhav:** Yehi iss poori kitaab ka sawal hai. Ek switch se kuch nahi hota. Do switch se thoda kuch hota hai. Arab switch se woh sab hota hai jo tumne aaj subah se ab tak kiya hai. Har photo, har message, har video, har jawab jo tumhe AI ne diya.

**Kabir:** Yeh maanna mushkil hai.

**Madhav:** Maanna mat. Hum ise banayenge. Agle kuch chapter mein tum khud dekh loge ki ek switch se do tak, do se ginti tak, ginti se faisle tak, aur faisle se har cheez tak kaise pahunchte hain. Us din tumhe maanne ki zaroorat nahi padegi.

**Kabir:** Aur beech mein kuch aur bhi hai? Ya bas switch hi switch?

**Madhav:** Bas switch. Poori kitaab mein hum ek bhi nayi cheez neeche nahi daalenge. Jo bhi aayega, wahi arab switch alag tareeke se jude honge.

---

### Naam

Woh chhota switch jo chip ke andar hota hai, use **transistor** kehte hain.

Woh deewar wale switch se sirf teen tarah se alag hai:

```
DEEWAR KA SWITCH                TRANSISTOR
haath se dabana padta hai   →   bijli se hi dabta hai
ek second mein ek baar      →   ek second mein arbon baar
ek ungli jitna bada         →   itna chhota ki dikhta hi nahi
```

Teesri baat sabse zaroori hai, aur woh yeh hai: **ek transistor ko doosra transistor daba sakta hai.**

Iska matlab hai ki switch ek doosre ko control kar sakte hain, bina kisi insaan ke. Aur wahi cheez sab kuch mumkin banati hai. Aage do chapter isi ek baat pe hain.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

Pehla transistor 1947 mein bana, Bell Labs mein. Woh haath mein pakadne laayak tha.

1971 mein Intel ne ek chip banayi jismein 2,300 transistor the.

Aaj ek aam phone ki chip mein lagbhag 15 se 20 arab transistor hote hain.

Ginti karo: 2,300 se 20,00,00,00,000 tak, pachas saal mein. Aur unmein se har ek wahi ek kaam karta hai jo deewar wala switch karta hai.

Poori computer ki duniya mein jo kuch bhi hua hai, woh iss ek cheez ke chhota aur sasta hone se hua hai. Koi naya idea nahi aaya. Wahi switch, bas zyada.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki computer ke andar kuch aur bhi hai.**

Log maante hain ki kahin koi jaadu hai. Koi aisi cheez jo sirf engineers ko pata hai. Kuch aisa jo samajh se bahar hai.

Andar kuch nahi hai. Sirf switch hain, aur woh ek doosre ko dabate hain.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai kyunki jo cheez computer karta hai woh switchon se bahut door lagti hai. Ek gaana, ek chehra, ek jawab. In cheezon aur "chalu ya band" ke beech ki doori itni badi hai ki dimaag maanne se mana kar deta hai.

Woh doori asli hai. Lekin usmein koi jaadu nahi hai, sirf parat pe parat hai. Aur hum har parat ko ek-ek karke rakhenge.

---

### Ek cheez jo poori kitaab mein chalegi

Iss kitaab mein hum ek chhote product ko saath lekar chalenge:

> **Ek chat app. Aapke saare messages sambhal ke rakhta hai, aur unmein se kuch bhi dhoondh sakta hai.**

Bas itna. Sunne mein lagta hai ek aadmi ek hafte mein bana le.

Har part ke aakhir mein hum iss app pe wapas aayenge, aur har baar uske andar ek nayi parat khulegi. Abhi ke liye sirf ek sawal apne dimaag mein rakh lo:

**Jab aap ek message type karte ho, woh message jaata kahan hai?**

Kitaab ke aakhir tak aapko iska poora jawab pata hoga, upar se neeche tak, aur yeh bhi ki aisa app banane mein kitna waqt lagta.

---

### Map pe

```
    Naksha yahan se shuru hota hai.

    ┌────────────────────────────────────┐
    │            ?                       │
    │            ?                       │   sab kuch abhi khaali hai
    │            ?                       │
    ├────────────────────────────────────┤
    │  SWITCH: juda hua ya toota hua     │  ← sirf yeh rakha hai
    │          1        ya      0        │
    └────────────────────────────────────┘

    Iske neeche kuch nahi jayega.
    Poori kitaab isi ke upar banegi.
```

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Ek switch ki do haalatein hain. Do switch ko saath rakho. Ab kitni alag haalatein ban sakti hain? Teen switch se?

> **Jawab:** Do switch se chaar. Teen se aath.
>
> Ginti aise karo: pehla switch do haal mein ho sakta hai, aur uski har haalat mein doosra bhi do haal mein ho sakta hai. Toh 2 x 2 = 4. Teesra aaya toh phir se dugna, 8.
>
> Yeh chhota sa hisaab poori kitaab ka sabse taakatwar hisaab hai. Har naye switch pe **dugna**. Das switch se 1,024. Bees se dus lakh se zyada.
>
> Isiliye arab switch itni badi cheez hai. Aur isiliye ek transistor ka chhota hona itna mayne rakhta hai.

**2. (samajh check)** Madhav ne kaha ki ek transistor ko doosra transistor daba sakta hai, aur yeh sabse zaroori baat hai. Deewar wale switch ke saath aisa nahi ho sakta. Kyun mayne rakhta hai ki ek switch doosre ko dabaye?

> **Jawab:** Kyunki tab insaan ki zaroorat khatam ho jaati hai.
>
> Deewar wale switch ko har baar koi ungli chahiye. Yaani woh utni hi baar chal sakta hai jitni baar koi aadmi use dabaye.
>
> Jab ek switch doosre ko dabata hai, toh ek switch dabane se ek poori shrinkhla chal padti hai, aur uske liye kisi ki zaroorat nahi. Aap ek baar tay kar dete ho ki kaun kise dabayega, aur uske baad machine khud chalti hai.
>
> **Wahi "khud chalna" hi poori technology hai.** Chapter 0.2 ki bhasha mein: yahi Anil ki naali hai. Ek baar bana do, phir woh aapke bina chalti rahe.

**3. (jodne wala)** Chapter 0.4 kehta tha ki har technology apne neeche wali cheez ko chhupa deti hai. Aapne pandrah saal phone istemaal kiya aur aaj tak transistor ke baare mein nahi socha. Kya yeh phone ki kami hai ya uski kaamyaabi?

> **Jawab:** Uski kaamyaabi. Poori tarah se.
>
> Agar phone istemaal karne ke liye aapko transistor samajhna padta, toh woh ek bekaar phone hota.
>
> Lekin ab woh doosri baat bhi yaad karo jo Kabir ne 0.4 mein khud nikaali thi: chhupi hui cheez ko aap istemaal kar sakte ho, uske upar kuch bana nahi sakte.
>
> Isliye phone theek hai. Sirf aapka kaam ab yeh hai ki uss deewar mein ek darwaza banao.

# Chapter 1.2  [SPINE]
## Yeh maan kar chal raha hoon

---

### Samvaad

**Kabir:** Ek baat poochun? Iske liye mujhe maths aani chahiye?

**Madhav:** Kitni aati hai?

**Kabir:** School wali. Uske baad chhoot gayi.

**Madhav:** Do aur do?

**Kabir:** Chaar.

**Madhav:** Aadha kitna hota hai?

**Kabir:** Sau ka pachas.

**Madhav:** Bas. Poori kitaab ke liye itni kaafi hai.

**Kabir:** Sach mein? Computer toh poora maths hi hai.

**Madhav:** Computer ke andar maths hai. Use samajhne ke liye maths nahi chahiye. Yeh do alag baatein hain, aur log inhe hamesha mila dete hain.

**Kabir:** Farak samjhao.

**Madhav:** Tumhare pet ke andar kya ho raha hai abhi?

**Kabir:** Khaana pach raha hai.

**Madhav:** Usmein kaunse kemikal lag rahe hain, bata sakte ho?

**Kabir:** Nahi.

**Madhav:** Phir bhi tum yeh samajhte ho ki khaana kaise pachta hai. Khaya, tuta, sharir ne le liya, bacha hua nikal gaya. Yeh samajh galat hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Bas gehri nahi hai.

**Madhav:** Woh galat nahi hai. Woh **ek parat ki** samajh hai. Usse aap khaane ke faisle le sakte ho, doctor se baat kar sakte ho, aur yeh samajh sakte ho ki bimari kyun hui. Kemikal ki zaroorat sirf tab padti hai jab tum nayi dawai banao.

**Kabir:** Toh yeh kitaab mujhe kis parat pe le jaayegi?

**Madhav:** Utni gehri jitni faisle lene ke liye chahiye. Tum yeh samajh jaoge ki har cheez kaam kaise karti hai aur kyun waisi hai. Tum yeh nahi seekhoge ki use banayein kaise.

**Kabir:** Toh main code likhna nahi seekhunga.

**Madhav:** Nahi. Aur yeh main pehle din bata raha hoon, taaki teesre hafte mein tumhe dhokha na lage.

**Kabir:** Toh phir faayda kya hai?

**Madhav:** Do aadmi socho. Ek ko code likhna aata hai lekin naksha nahi pata. Doosre ko naksha pata hai lekin code nahi aata. Kaunsa aadmi yeh tay kar sakta hai ki kya banaya jaaye?

**Kabir:** Doosra.

**Madhav:** Aur kaunsa ek hafte mein code seekh sakta hai, kyunki uske paas har cheez ke liye jagah pehle se hai?

**Kabir:** Doosra hi.

**Madhav:** Ulta karna mushkil hai. Code se naksha nahi banta. Log saalon code likhte hain aur naksha phir bhi nahi hota.

**Kabir:** Aur mujhe kya kya pehle se aana chahiye?

**Madhav:** Teen cheezein. Padhna aata ho. Ginti aati ho. Aur tumne kabhi phone ya computer istemaal kiya ho.

**Kabir:** Bas?

**Madhav:** Bas. Aur ek cheez jo maine maan li hai aur woh hunar nahi hai: har chapter ke baad tum ek minute ruk kar sochoge. Agar woh nahi karoge toh yeh kitaab kaam nahi karegi, chahe tumhe kitni bhi maths aati ho.

---

### Naam

Yeh chapter iss kitaab ka **contract** hai. Dono taraf se.

**Jo maan kar chal raha hoon:**

```
aap padh sakte ho
aap gin sakte ho, aur aadha-dugna samajhte ho
aapne kabhi phone ya computer istemaal kiya hai
aap har chapter ke baad ek minute rukoge
```

**Jo nahi maan raha:**

```
aapko maths aati hai
aapne pehle kabhi coding ki hai
aap kisi technical shabd ka matlab jaante ho
aapko yaad hai school mein kya padha tha
```

**Jo yeh kitaab degi:**

```
har parat kaam kaise karti hai, aur kyun waisi hai
kisi bhi nayi cheez ko naksh e pe rakhne ki taakat
ek product ko kaagaz pe design karne ki samajh
AI se woh nikaalna jo expert nikaalte hain
```

**Jo yeh kitaab nahi degi:**

```
code likhne ki kabiliyat
kisi language mein maharat
haath se kaam karne ka tajurba
dikhane laayak koi banaya hua saaman
```

Samajh pehle aati hai aur use chhoda nahi ja sakta. Kabiliyat alag cheez hai, woh karne se aati hai, aur woh iske baad aati hai.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

Ek building ka architect aur ek mistri.

Architect ne shayad zindagi mein ek deewar nahi banayi. Woh cement nahi ghol sakta, sariya nahi bandh sakta. Us hisaab se woh mistri se kam kaabil hai.

Lekin architect yeh tay karta hai ki building kaisi hogi, kitni manzil sahi hai, kahan wazan padega, kahan roshni aayegi, aur kya cheez giregi.

Mistri ko yeh sab nahi aata, aur usne poori zindagi deewarein banayi hain.

Dono kaam ke hain. Dono ko paisa milta hai. Lekin building ki shakal architect tay karta hai.

Yeh kitaab aapko architect banati hai. Mistri banna alag padhai hai, aur bahut aasan ho jaati hai jab naksha pehle se dimaag mein ho.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki samajhne se pehle karna zaroori hai.**

"Pehle coding seekh lo, phir sab samajh aa jaayega." Yeh salaah har jagah milti hai.

Woh kabhi kabhi kaam karti hai, aur bahut mehngi hai. Kyunki bina naksha ke code seekhne ka matlab hai hazaaron chhote tukde yaad karna bina yeh jaane ki woh kis cheez ka hissa hain. Log saal-do-saal lagate hain aur unhe abhi bhi nahi pata ki jo cheez woh likh rahe hain woh kahan chal rahi hai.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai ki karne se **turant kuch dikhta hai**. Pehle din kuch chal jaata hai screen pe. Samajhne mein pehle hafte kuch nahi dikhta.

Aur ek aur wajah: karne ki salaah dene wale zyadatar log woh hain jinke paas naksha **already hai**. Unhe yaad nahi ki woh unhe kab aur kaise mila, isliye woh maan lete hain ki woh karne se hi aa gaya tha.

---

### Map pe

```
    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │   KABILIYAT     code likhna, banana     │  ← alag padhai
    ├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │   SAMAJH        naksha, kya kahan hai   │  ← yeh kitaab
    ├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │   SWITCH        1 ya 0                  │  ← kal rakha tha
    └─────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Samajh ke bina kabiliyat = tukde
    Kabiliyat ke bina samajh = design
    Dono = builder
```

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Madhav ne kaha ki khaana pachne ki "ek parat ki samajh" galat nahi hai, bas gehri nahi hai. Aisi teen cheezein batao jo aap roz istemaal karte ho aur jinki aapko ek parat ki samajh hai. Kya kabhi us kami se aapka kaam ruka?

> **Jawab:** Aam jawab: gaadi, bijli, bank, dawai, internet.
>
> Aur zyadatar logon ke liye kaam kabhi nahi rukta, kyunki woh un cheezon ko sirf **istemaal** karte hain.
>
> Kaam tab rukta hai jab aap kuch chunna chahte ho ya banana chahte ho. Do dawaiyon mein se behtar chunni ho. Do phone mein se behtar chunna ho. Kisi ko batana ho ki kya banaya jaaye.
>
> Isse ek saaf rule nikalta hai: **jab tak aap sirf istemaal kar rahe ho, ek parat kaafi hai. Jis din chunna ya banana ho, us din nahi.**

**2. (samajh check)** Kitaab kehti hai ki woh coding nahi sikhayegi. Kabir ne poocha "toh faayda kya hai." Ab aap uska jawab do, apne shabdon mein, ek line mein.

> **Jawab:** Kai theek jawab hain. Sabse mazboot yeh hai:
>
> **Code likhna aaj sabse sasti cheez hai. Yeh jaanna ki kya likhna chahiye, sabse mehngi.**
>
> Aur Chapter 0.2 ki bhasha mein: code likhna ek hunar hai jo lakhon logon ke paas hai, toh uska scarcity dial neeche hai. Yeh tay karna ki kya banaya jaaye, woh bahut kam log kar paate hain.
>
> Ek aur seedha jawab: is kitaab ke baad koi bhi nayi technology aayegi toh aapko uske liye jagah dhoondhni nahi padegi. Woh khabar nahi lagegi.

**3. (jodne wala)** Chapter 0.5 mein Kabir ne kaha tha ki tukdon mein seekhne se har cheez "khabar" lagti hai. Iss chapter ka architect wala example uss baat se kaise judta hai?

> **Jawab:** Architect ke paas ek dhaancha hai: wazan, roshni, jagah, kharcha. Jab woh koi nayi building dekhta hai, toh use nayi cheez nahi dikhti. Use dikhta hai ki wahi jaani-pehchani cheezein iss baar kaise jodi gayi hain.
>
> Mistri jo sirf deewarein banata aaya hai, use har nayi design ek nayi cheez lagti hai.
>
> Wahi farak Kabir ka hai. Jab dhaancha nahi hota toh har cheez alag se aati hai aur alag se bhoolni padti hai. Jab dhaancha hota hai toh nayi cheez sirf ek farak hoti hai, poori cheez nahi.

# Chapter 1.3  [SPINE]
## Computer actually hai kya

---

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ek switch ki do haalatein hain. Ab do switch ek taar mein ek ke baad ek lagao. Bulb jalega kab?

**Kabir:** Jab dono chalu hon. Ek bhi band hua toh raasta toota.

**Madhav:** Aur agar dono switch alag-alag raaston pe hon, aur dono raaste bulb tak jaate hon?

**Kabir:** Toh ek bhi chalu ho toh bulb jal jaayega.

**Madhav:** Ruko. Tumne abhi jo bola, use dhyaan se suno. Pehle case mein tumne kya kaha?

**Kabir:** Dono chalu hon tabhi.

**Madhav:** Aur doosre mein?

**Kabir:** Ek bhi chalu ho toh kaafi.

**Madhav:** Tumne abhi do faisle banaye. Sirf taar jodne ke tareeke se. "Dono" aur "koi ek." Yeh sirf bijli nahi hai. Yeh ek shart hai.

**Kabir:** Theek hai, lekin yeh toh bahut chhoti baat hai.

**Madhav:** Ek teesra tareeka bhi hai. Aisa switch socho jo ulta chalta hai. Bijli aaye toh raasta tode, na aaye toh jode.

**Kabir:** Woh kis kaam ka?

**Madhav:** Woh "nahi" hai. Ab tumhare paas teen cheezein hain: **dono**, **koi ek**, aur **nahi**. Ab main tumse ek badi baat kahunga aur tum use abhi maanoge nahi. In teen cheezon se, kaafi saari lagakar, tum har woh sawal hal kar sakte ho jiska jawab haan ya na mein ho.

**Kabir:** Har sawal? Jaise?

**Madhav:** Jaise: yeh do numbers barabar hain? Yeh number us number se bada hai? Yeh tareekh guzar chuki hai? Yeh password sahi hai?

**Kabir:** Yeh sab sirf "dono, koi ek, nahi" se?

**Madhav:** Sirf isse. Ek udaharan lo. Do switch, A aur B. Main jaanna chahta hoon ki dono alag hain ya nahi. Batao kaise pata karoge.

**Kabir:** Hmm. Agar A chalu hai aur B band, toh alag hain. Ya A band aur B chalu, tab bhi alag hain.

**Madhav:** Toh usko teen cheezon mein likho.

**Kabir:** "A aur B-nahi" wala case. Ya "A-nahi aur B" wala case. Koi ek ho jaaye toh alag hain.

**Madhav:** Tumne abhi taaron se tulna banayi. Kisi ne tumhe formula nahi diya. Ab yeh socho: agar do cheezein barabar hain ya nahi, yeh switchon se pata chal sakta hai, toh kya cheez nahi chal sakti?

**Kabir:** Pata nahi. Shayad kuch bhi nahi.

**Madhav:** Ab teesri baat, aur yeh sabse zaroori hai. Pichhle chapter mein maine kaha tha ki ek transistor doosre ko daba sakta hai. Iska matlab kya hua?

**Kabir:** Ki ek shart ka jawab doosri shart mein daala ja sakta hai.

**Madhav:** Aur uska jawab teesri mein. Aur aage. Kitni door tak?

**Kabir:** Jitne switch hon.

**Madhav:** Aur tumhare phone mein kitne hain?

**Kabir:** Bees arab.

**Madhav:** Toh tumhare paas bees arab shartein hain, ek doosre mein daali hui, aur woh ek second mein arbon baar chal sakti hain. Ab batao, computer kya hai?

**Kabir:** Switch ka dhher, jo ek doosre ko dabate hain, aur milkar shartein banate hain.

**Madhav:** Aur uske alaawa?

**Kabir:** Kuch nahi?

**Madhav:** Kuch nahi. Ab poori kitaab mein hum yehi karenge: dekhte jaayenge ki iske upar parat pe parat kaise chadhi.

---

### Naam

Woh teen cheezein jo Kabir ne banayi, unke naam hain **AND**, **OR** aur **NOT**.

```
AND    dono chalu hon tabhi        (ek ke baad ek taar)
OR     koi ek chalu ho toh kaafi   (alag alag raaste)
NOT    ulta kar do                 (chalu ho toh band)
```

Inhe **logic gates** kehte hain. Gate isliye ki woh bijli ko rok bhi sakte hain aur jaane bhi de sakte hain, shart ke hisaab se.

Aur jo baat Kabir ne khud nikaali, uska naam bhi hai. Yeh saabit ho chuka hai ki **in teen se** har woh cheez banayi ja sakti hai jo koi bhi computer karta hai. Aur jodna, ghatana, tulna, yaad rakhna, sab.

Toh ek computer ki poori definition itni hai:

> Bahut saare switch, aise jude hue ki woh AND, OR aur NOT banayein, aur ek ka jawab doosre mein jaaye.

Iske alaawa computer mein kuch nahi hai. Baaki sab jo aap dekhte ho, wo iske upar chadhi hui parat hai.

---

### Asli duniya se ek example

1937 mein ek 21 saal ka student, Claude Shannon, ne apni master's thesis likhi. Usne dikhaya ki bijli ke switchon ke jaal aur tark ke niyam bilkul ek jaisi cheez hain.

Us waqt switch phone ke exchange mein lagte the, call jodne ke liye. Kisi ne unhe sochne wali cheez nahi maana tha.

Shannon ne dikhaya ki agar aap switch ko tark ki tarah likh sakte ho, toh aap tark ko switch se bana bhi sakte ho.

Woh thesis lagbhag har digital cheez ki neev hai jo uske baad bani. Aur usmein koi nayi machine nahi thi, sirf yeh dekhna tha ki do purani cheezein ek hi cheez hain.

---

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki computer "sochta" hai.**

Log kehte hain computer ne decide kiya, computer ko laga, computer samajh gaya. Yeh sirf bolne ka tareeka nahi hai, log sach mein aisa mehsoos karte hain.

Computer koi faisla nahi leta. Usmein bees arab shartein pehle se lagi hui hain, aur bijli un shartों mein se guzar jaati hai. Jo bahar aata hai, woh jawab hai.

Ek chaabi wale taale se socho. Sahi chaabi ghumti hai, galat nahi ghumti. Taale ne kuch soch kar faisla nahi kiya. Uski shakal aisi hai. Computer ek taala hai jismein bees arab pins hain aur unhe har second badla ja sakta hai.

Yeh galti tempting isliye hai ki bahar se dono ek jaise dikhte hain. Aap sawal poochte ho, jawab aata hai. Andar kya hua, woh dikhta nahi.

Yeh baat Book 2 mein bahut kaam aayegi, jab hum AI kholenge. Wahan yeh galti sabse zyada hoti hai.

---

### Map pe

```
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  SHARTEIN     AND, OR, NOT             │  ← naya
    │               ek ka jawab doosre mein  │
    ├────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │  SWITCH       1 ya 0                   │
    └────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### Sochne ke liye

Har sawal pe ek minute socho, phir neeche jawab padho.

**1. (samajh check)** Ek ghar mein aisa taala lagana hai jo tabhi khule jab do log ek saath apni chaabi lagayein. Kaunsa gate hai yeh? Ab aisa taala jo kisi ek chaabi se bhi khul jaaye?

> **Jawab:** Pehla AND hai. Doosra OR.
>
> Bank ki tijoriyon mein sach mein AND lagta hai: do alag logon ki chaabi, ek saath. Ghar ke darwaze pe OR hota hai: aapki chaabi ya ghar ke kisi aur ki, dono chalti hain.
>
> Dhyaan do ki yeh koi computer ki cheez nahi hai. Yeh sirf shart hai. Computer ne shart invent nahi ki, usne use bijli mein daal diya.

**2. (samajh check)** Madhav ne kaha ki AND, OR aur NOT se har haan-na wala sawal hal ho sakta hai. Ek aisa sawal sochne ki koshish karo jo iss tareeke se hal na ho.

> **Jawab:** Agar sawal ka jawab pakka haan ya na hai, aur jo cheezein us jawab ko tay karti hain woh sab pata hain, toh woh hal ho jaayega. Iska koi apwaad nahi hai. Yeh saabit ho chuka hai.
>
> Jo sawal hal nahi honge, woh do tarah ke hain:
>
> **Jinka jawab haan-na mein hai hi nahi.** "Yeh gaana achha hai?" Yahan pehle yeh tay karna padega ki achhe ka matlab kya hai. Woh tay ho jaaye toh sawal hal ho jaayega, lekin jawab aapki di hui paribhasha ka hoga, sachai ka nahi.
>
> **Jinka jawab tay karne wali cheezein pata hi nahi hain.** "Kal baarish hogi?" Yeh switch ki kami nahi hai, yeh jaankari ki kami hai.
>
> Yeh farak yaad rakhna. Book 2 mein AI ki lagbhag saari seemayein inhi do mein se ek hain.

**3. (jodne wala)** Chapter 1.1 mein aapne dekha ki har naye switch pe haalatein dugni ho jaati hain. Ab jodo: agar bees arab switch hain, toh kya sach mein har mumkin haalat kabhi istemaal hoti hai?

> **Jawab:** Nahi, bilkul nahi. Aur farak kalpana se bahar hai.
>
> Bees arab switch se jitni haalatein banti hain, woh brahmand ke saare paramanuon se bhi behisaab zyada hain. Unmein se ek bahut hi chhota hissa hi kabhi kaam mein aata hai.
>
> Toh phir itne switch kyun? Kyunki aapko pehle se nahi pata ki kaunsi haalat chahiye hogi. Machine ko har mumkin cheez ke liye taiyaar rehna padta hai, isliye nahi ki sab hongi, balki isliye ki koi bhi ho sakti hai.
>
> Yeh soch aage kai jagah dikhegi: **jagah isliye nahi banayi jaati ki woh bhar jaayegi, balki isliye ki pehle se pata nahi ki kya aayega.**

---

# Chapter 1.4  [SPINE]
## Switches numbers kaise rakhte hain

---

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ek switch se kitne numbers dikha sakte ho?

**Kabir:** Do. 0 aur 1.

**Madhav:** Do switch se?

**Kabir:** Chaar haalatein hain. Toh 0, 1, 2, 3.

**Madhav:** Likh ke dikhao. Kaunsi haalat kaunsa number hai?

**Kabir:** Dono band ko 0 kehta hoon. Phir 01 ko 1. Phir 10 ko 2. Phir 11 ko 3.

**Madhav:** Tumne yeh order kyun chuna?

**Kabir:** Bas... ginti jaisi lagi.

**Madhav:** Woh ginti jaisi hai. Ab teen switch se ginti karo, 0 se shuru.

**Kabir:** 000, 001, 010, 011, 100, 101, 110, 111. Aath haalatein. 0 se 7.

**Madhav:** Aur tumne ginti kaise ki? Kya niyam laga rahe the?

**Kabir:** Dayen se badhata gaya. Jab 1 ke aage badhna tha toh use 0 kar ke agle ko 1 kar diya.

**Madhav:** Bilkul wahi jo tum aam ginti mein karte ho. 9 ke baad 0 likh kar aage 1 badhate ho. Sirf yahan 9 ki jagah 1 pe hi bharav ho jaata hai, kyunki sirf do nishaan hain.

**Kabir:** Toh yeh koi nayi ginti nahi hai. Bas kam nishaan hain.

**Madhav:** Bas itna hi. Aam ginti mein das nishaan hain, 0 se 9. Yahan do hain. Aur kuch nahi badla.

**Kabir:** Lekin fayda kya hua? 7 likhne ke liye teen switch lag gaye. 7 toh ek nishaan hai.

**Madhav:** Sahi sawal. Ab dhyaan se dekho. Teen switch se 8 haalatein. Chaar se?

**Kabir:** Solah.

**Madhav:** Aath switch se?

**Kabir:** 2 ko aath baar guna... 256.

**Madhav:** Solah switch se?

**Kabir:** 65,000 ke aas-paas.

**Madhav:** Tees switch se?

**Kabir:** Bahut zyada. Karodon.

**Madhav:** Ek arab se upar. Ab yeh dekho: switch tees hain, lekin numbers ek arab. Har naya switch **dugna** karta hai. Isiliye tumhara sawal ulta ho gaya. Sawal yeh nahi hai ki 7 ke liye teen switch kyun lage. Sawal yeh hai ki tees switch se ek arab kaise aa gaye.

**Kabir:** Theek hai. Toh numbers ban gaye. Lekin mujhe toh naam bhi likhne hain, gaane bhi.

**Madhav:** Ek kaam karo. Ek kaagaz pe likho: A ke liye 1, B ke liye 2, C ke liye 3. Aage bhi.

**Kabir:** Kar liya.

**Madhav:** Ab "CAB" likho, sirf numbers mein.

**Kabir:** 3, 1, 2.

**Madhav:** Aur maine tumhe woh kaagaz diya hota, toh main 3-1-2 padh kar kya samajhta?

**Kabir:** CAB.
